Named checks before it say which premise a change breaks: the statements up to and including `import hashlib` (`openssl_removal_differs`), no new import, no write of the environment after them (including `getattr` by name, namespaces and dynamic code), no new `env=` key, no new program start. `floor_problems` runs that static read before `lists_differ`, which runs the commit's code. *Run:* 1839 tests on Python 3.13 (1838 before); 38 mutant commits refused at HEAD, each by its named check, and all 38 passing with this slice's checks stubbed out; 4 harmless edits let through; `obaaf docs build --check`, `obaaf catalog`, `obaaf check . --fail-on medium` exit 0; this repository and Expectrail at `f7e11102` (a copy without `fresh-*`, its own CI command) byte-identical before and after; all 13 checks green on `5b45078` (CI [43848880772](https://github.com/Obaaf/OBAAF/actions/runs/43848880772), escape tests [43848880815](https://github.com/Obaaf/OBAAF/actions/runs/43848880815)). *Review:* five passes by Opus 5.5 sub-agents that did not write it. Passes 1 and 2 were each stopped by a safety classifier while they wrote edits to get past the guard; their other findings (three citations; an earlier step's computed `env`) were fixed, and the run did the search itself, finding 8 ways past its first guard (a `getattr` chain, `__builtins__`, `__subclasses__`, an `env=` dict naming `OPENSSL_CONF` or `LD_PRELOAD`, one more start), all fixed; that part was checked only by its author. Pass 3, asked to read the guard without writing such edits, found 4 blocking (`_quiet`'s `extra_env`, new arguments to a start, `getattr`/`eval` under other names, `OBAAF_EGRESS_SCRIPT` in `action.yml`: so the whole-action identity check, F-180; a wrong count; mutants that tripped two checks; and the `GITHUB_ENV` reason contradicting literal `env` keys being verified). Pass 4 found 2 (the `OPENSSL_` refusal said of every version, which changed a finding; a coding declaration). Pass 5 found none. *Not done:* #197 (the action's refusal message), #198 (2c.12), #194; the bytes-versus-text backstop has no mutant of its own; `action.yml` is compared as PyYAML reads it. Guide: [egress-control.md](egress-control.md) item 12.
- ☑ **2c.12 Which variables set for the egress action's own steps still change what they do with the key** (core; issues #198 and #194, both closed; decision D-075, proposed; F-194 to F-198). Merged on 2026-11-17 in two parts: **2c.12a**, the measurement, as [#203](https://github.com/Obaaf/OBAAF/pull/203) (merge `02a7efb`; started by run `scaup`, finished by run `whimbrel`), and **2c.12b**, the action's change with the floor, as [#206](https://github.com/Obaaf/OBAAF/pull/206) (merge `68c4e3f`; run `whimbrel`, in Sean's session, item 52). *What was measured (2c.12a):* two escape-test jobs set 41 names through the job's `env`, a step's `env` and `GITHUB_ENV` and record what a step run as the action's steps are sees (`tests/egress/runner-variables.json`, required on every run of the escape tests). The runner keeps its own `RUNNER_TEMP`, `GITHUB_WORKSPACE` and the other runner directories and files, so a job cannot move the action's state directory; `NODE_OPTIONS` reaches a step from an `env` but not through `GITHUB_ENV`; every other name reaches the step with the workflow's value. *What the steps did with them (2c.12b):* on GitHub-hosted `ubuntu-24.04` the action at `cc2ba59` opened the locale `LC_ALL` named and the file `TZ` named as Python started, and its download went to the proxy the workflow named and read the CA file it named. None is known to hand over the key (a locale can only be one under `/usr/lib/locale`; the two files are parsed, not run; the download is hash-checked), but each is the workflow choosing what a key-holding process reads. *What was built:* each of the three steps' own `env` sets `LC_ALL: C` and `TZ: UTC0`, which are read before `egress.py`'s first line; `egress.py` copies its environment and empties it before it imports anything but `os`, reads its inputs from the copy, refuses to go on if either value is not the pinned one, and refuses `LC_ALL` and `TZ` under its `env` input (the step sees the action's values, not the workflow's). `run()`, AWF's environment and the command's are unchanged. `tests/egress/environment-watch.py` and the escape tests' `own-steps-environment` job plant a file or directory for each variable to name, watch them with inotify and listen on the proxy's port: a plain step and the action at `cc2ba59` used the locale, the zone, the CA file and the proxy, and the action now used none and still confined its command ([run 43988832906](https://github.com/Obaaf/OBAAF/actions/runs/43988832906)). `egress_action_floor` moves from `cc2ba59` to `f3b50d4`; the listing guard gains named checks for the slice's premises and 12 more mutant commits (69 in all). No variable is added to what `key-out-of-reach` counts and none is taken away. *Issue #194:* not reachable at AWF v0.29.13. A control shows `docker` running a plug-in from a home that holds one; the plug-in the confined command wrote into its home was not run, was nowhere on the runner afterwards, and was not in the home AWF's `docker` reads. The action is not changed for it (F-196). *Reviews:* each part by an Opus 5.5 sub-agent that did not write it, in two passes; nothing blocking in any. 2c.12a: five corrections, then three. 2c.12b: four (a positive control for #194, an ordering assumption in the job, a title that over-claimed, three claims that were reasoned and not measured), then one sentence. No reviewer was stopped. *Before and after:* this repository and Expectrail at `5d25639`, with its CI command, give byte-identical text and JSON reports; no commit of the action is listed. 1911 tests; all 18 checks green on `aff70f4`. *Not done:* runners other than `ubuntu-24.04`; a real agent or model key; what the action at `cc2ba59` read after its download (the step failed at the planted proxy); the start-up reading rests on a trace of files opened, which would not show a variable that changes start-up without opening one. *Left open, for Sean (item 55):* whether a write to `GITHUB_ENV` and `ACTIONS_ALLOW_UNSECURE_COMMANDS` still need to give "cannot tell" (F-198). The loader variables the `env` input passes on empty are issue #208.

**2d. Hardening**

- ☑ **2d.1 Expressions are read from their tokens and fail closed.** Merged as [#53](https://github.com/Obaaf/OBAAF/pull/53) and [#54](https://github.com/Obaaf/OBAAF/pull/54). Before it, secrets and outsider-written fields were found by matching patterns against an expression's text, and these spellings got past every rule: `secrets.*` and any use of the `secrets` context other than one literal name; a secret in `outputs` or any job key outside a fixed list; anything after a `}}` inside a string; and for OBAAF-GHA-010 a wildcard, a computed index, a group or function result, env values (through any number of hops), and the matrix. `obaaf.checker.expr` now tokenizes expressions; anything it cannot pin down counts as the worst case; files it cannot read are errors. An independent reviewer made three passes: the first found a crash and a slow-input regression in the first commit and two more bypass classes, the second found a memory blow-up and one more bypass in the fixes, the third found nothing blocking. Its fuzzing on the final code (200,000 generated expressions, 600,000 random token texts) found no missed secret read and no missed expansion; the reviewer notes that its oracle was aligned with three deliberate changes. 499 tests pass on the merged `flagship` (437 before), run locally on Python 3.13; the 116 checker tests also pass on Python 3.11. CI passed on #53's commit; **CI never ran on #54's commits**: its run was still queued, with no job started, when #54 was merged ten minutes after it was opened. The next standing pull request into `main` is where CI first runs on this code, on Python 3.10 among others. Output for existing fixtures, templates and Expectrail is unchanged. **Not run on GitHub**: what the runner does with these spellings rests on its documentation and on recollection of its source.
- ☑ **2d.2a Lane assignment.** Merged as [#62](https://github.com/Obaaf/OBAAF/pull/62); decision D-039 in `DECISIONS.md`; every case is in the changelog section "Lanes". Raised by the independent reviewer of slice 1.4 on 2026-11-11 with two cases: a job that runs an agent and publishes got the agent rules only, and a job declared an agent job that publishes got the release rules only. The cause was one lane per job, chosen in a fixed order. *What changed:* a job is in every lane that applies and gets the rules of each; a declaration adds a lane and never removes one; a declaration that does nothing is listed in the notes; a release environment makes a release job whatever can start it; the Jenkins analyser follows the same rule for the declarations that reach a step, and reports OBAAF-JEN-012 on the step that nothing checks. *How it was checked:* 66 tests, one for each case; the same findings and exit codes as before on this repository, the 15 risky workflows and Expectrail's pinned configuration at two commits (one new note for Expectrail, whose configuration gives a job the lane `probe`); and five passes by a reviewer that did not write it, comparing the checker before the slice with each commit on 18,480 generated workflows and 14,400 Jenkinsfile and configuration cases. The last pass found nothing lower than before, no declaration that removes a finding and no crash, and five sentences that said more than the code does, which were corrected. Every earlier pass found something, twice in the fix for the pass before it (decision F-28). *One reduction, on purpose:* an environment named `nonprod`, `preprod` or `pre-production` is no longer taken for a release environment. *Done when* a reviewer who did not write the fix has attacked lane assignment, every case found is a test, the fix removes the class and not the case, existing fixtures and Expectrail's pinned configuration give results that are no weaker than before, and the details are published with the fix.
- ☐ **2d.2 Remaining Jenkins rule correctness** (#78). After 2d.2j, check OBAAF-JEN-005 and 009 through 012 against documented behavior. Done when regression fixtures check expected findings and errors, the fixes pass the suite, and the guide states remaining limits.
- ☑ **2d.2b What a job can reach.** Merged as [#157](https://github.com/Obaaf/OBAAF/pull/157) by run `knot` on 2026-11-14; split from 2d.2 (F-124); decision D-058 in `DECISIONS.md` (proposed); the record is [bypass-attempts.md](bypass-attempts.md); every change an adopter will see is in the changelog under "What a job can reach: six rules attacked" and in the upgrade note. *What was tried:* two Opus 5.5 sub-agents that did not write the rules wrote about 115 test workflows against OBAAF-GHA-001, 003, 004, 005, 018 and 024. *What got through, and the fix to each class:* undocumented `permissions` spellings (another case, `WRITE`, `" write"`, `Write-All`, an expression) were read as no permissions with no word, and are now an error for the file read as the widest grant; a job's `permissions:` with no value hid a workflow-level `write-all`; a called workflow's agent job (`workflow_call`) holds its caller's token and is now reported as `inherited` by OBAAF-GHA-003 and 004; an environment named by an expression lowered OBAAF-GHA-001 to medium; OBAAF-GHA-024 ignored `secrets: inherit`, let one `protected_secrets` entry replace another of different case, took names equal only under Unicode case folding for the protected one, and read numbers, blanks and lists as names; OBAAF-GHA-018 dropped every job that can read every secret (now `any-secret`) and missed continued lines and common commands, actions and names; an acceptance written for one environment covered another (the environment is now a fact of the secrets findings of OBAAF-GHA-001, 005 and 018); an event name in another case was dropped silently. *Review:* four passes by an Opus 5.5 sub-agent: three, three, one and no blocking findings; the description was not given to it in the first pass. *Checked:* 1452 tests pass locally; CI green on `e591362` ([run 43586592940](https://github.com/Obaaf/OBAAF/actions/runs/43586592940)); old against new checker on this repository, the templates, the risky fixtures and Expectrail at `5433b5e`: only the intended differences (an `environment` fact on 21 of Expectrail's findings), no count or exit status changed. *Left:* the caller of a local reusable agent workflow and an agent in a local composite action (2d.4); events an app can raise for an outsider (#158); OBAAF-GHA-018's command finding names the step and not its text; a constant expression environment is never evaluated. *Not run:* GitHub's own handling of the undocumented spellings and of numeric or empty environment names; the fixes are right either way.
- ☑ **2d.2c What a run checks out.** Merged as [#162](https://github.com/Obaaf/OBAAF/pull/162) by run `skua` on 2026-11-14; split from 2d.2 (F-126); decision D-060 in `DECISIONS.md` (proposed); the record is [bypass-attempts.md](bypass-attempts.md#2d2c-what-a-run-checks-out); every change an adopter will see is in the changelog under "What a run checks out: four rules attacked" and in the upgrade note. *What was tried:* an Opus 5.5 sub-agent that did not write the rules wrote 106 workflows against OBAAF-GHA-002, 011, 012 and 019; 57 got through, in 16 classes. *The fix to each class:* a checkout's `ref` and `repository` are read with the expression reader, `env` followed scope by scope, and a value the checker cannot read is "cannot tell" at medium (`checkout-unresolved`, GHA-012 `unresolved`); scripts are split into words as a shell splits them, command by command, with runners (`sudo`, `env`, `timeout`, `xargs`, `docker exec` ...) skipped, comments dropped, a quoted script given to `sh -c` read again, and scripts in Python, Node, PowerShell and github-script split roughly; a command that brings code in counts when the same command names the pull request's code, directly, through `env`, a variable, a written file or a git remote; GHA-012's merge commit covers the review events and `github.sha`; GHA-011 covers every job an outsider's event can start, reads `persist-credentials` as actions/checkout's v3.5.0 source does (later versions not read) and names the credential; GHA-019 counts any value but a plainly false one; new facts (what was read, the triggers, the `if:`, the credential, the value, and for "cannot tell" a digest of the workflow's and job's env, the job's strategy, its earlier steps and the jobs it needs); each finding 0.3.0 could make keeps the message 0.3.0 wrote for it (checked against the v0.1 fixtures, not against 0.3.0 itself), so v0.1 baselines still migrate. This repository's own escape-tests checkout that keeps its token is accepted in a new `.github/obaaf.yml` in Sean's name (F-128). *Review:* one Opus 5.5 sub-agent in four passes plus a last check, resumed with its context: 9, 7, 4, 1 and 0 blocking findings. Among them: a pattern that took 97 s on a 100 KB line and an env loop that took 83 s (now under a second), false positives on ordinary `pull_request_target` workflows, the commands of script languages, and three tracebacks (one older than the slice). *Checked:* 1506 tests pass locally (1460 at the start); CI green on every pushed head, last [43605136923](https://github.com/Obaaf/OBAAF/actions/runs/43605136923) on `5ea0841`; old against new checker on the 106 attempts (as the attacking reviewer counted them; 108 files) and the reviewing sub-agent's further 150 or so workflows, this repository, the templates, the risky fixtures and Expectrail at `9dc79d5` (read-only copy): only the intended differences; on Expectrail one new medium "cannot tell" (`expectrail-comment.yml#judge`, `ref: ${{ steps.start.outputs.base }}`). *Left:* local composite actions and reusable workflows (2d.4, in its entry); values passed through `GITHUB_ENV` or files; shell functions, arrays and aliases; `while read ... done < <(...)`; text in a bot's comment and the number in other arguments are still reported; an unquoted `$(...)` followed by a literal `git` subcommand is a rare false positive; a "cannot tell" acceptance reopens whenever an earlier step of the job changes; `pull_request_review(_comment)` counted as privileged by OBAAF-GHA-001 and 002 (a false positive, to look at in 2d.2d). *Not run:* GitHub's handling of the spellings the record marks "not verified"; actions/checkout's source after v3.5.0.
- ☑ **2d.2d Triggers, scripts and release jobs.** Merged as [#165](https://github.com/Obaaf/OBAAF/pull/165) by run `ruff` on 2026-11-14; split from 2d.2 by run `skua` (F-126); decision D-061 in `DECISIONS.md` (proposed); taken as 2d.2's first tranche with its starting points written (F-129). *What was built:* the classes from `skua`'s reviewer were fixed first; two Opus 5.5 reviewers then attacked the result in parallel (OBAAF-GHA-010: 85 attempts; 013 and 015: 64), 60 got through and 10 were false positives; every way past that is fixed is a test in `tests/test_trigger_script_release_bypasses.py` (41 tests), and every attempt, with what the checker reports for it now, is in [bypass-attempts.md](bypass-attempts.md#2d2d-triggers-scripts-and-release-jobs). OBAAF-GHA-010: more outsider-written fields (edited old values, transfers, review comment `path` and `diff_hunk`, committers and file lists, gollum titles, a discussion's answer, a fork's description, `workflow_run.head_repository` and `path`, merge group and check-event commits); scripts in `shell`, `defaults.run.shell`, the egress action's `run` under other spellings and the inputs under `script_inputs`; job `outputs` followed with a work list, and an `env:` written as one expression; high in a reusable workflow, on `commit_comment` and the check events, and for a commit's text on push, `merge_group` and a `workflow_run` after a push (F-132); facts `fields`, `expressions`, `triggers`; the 0.3.0 message kept as `legacy`, read with a copy of the old reader. OBAAF-GHA-013: `HOSTED_LABELS` written from what was known (F-133); `self-hosted` in any spelling; calling jobs skipped; only the `head.repo.full_name` selector informational, on pull request events and without an agent (F-130); the selector and matrix label sets are facts. OBAAF-GHA-015: more publishing commands and actions, read in `docker://` steps with continued lines joined and quotes removed; environment names matched anywhere after removing words that only hold the letters, plus short whole words, and resolved from a matrix; medium on pull request events only and with a plain-text environment, as OBAAF-GHA-001; facts reason, steps (by name and the command or action that publishes) and environment; new case `capability` (`packages: write`, publishing secrets, `publishing_actions`, an unreadable environment name; not `id-token: write` alone, F-131). *Run:* 1548 tests locally (1506 at the start); the new tests give 111 failures on the old checker; CI green on every head ([43618060830](https://github.com/Obaaf/OBAAF/actions/runs/43618060830) on the merged head `965d52a`); on Expectrail at `cb1fc85` (read-only copy, `fresh-*` left out unread) one false OBAAF-GHA-013 goes; on this repository two new low OBAAF-GHA-006 (`pages.yml#deploy`, `release-pypi.yml#publish` are now release jobs). *Review:* one Opus 5.5 sub-agent in four passes: 5 blocking (a traceback on a step name with braces, outputs followed in one order of jobs only, a 90 s cliff, production names lost, a false v0.1 claim), then 0, then 1 (unnamed release steps shared one fact), then 0; the last commit is wording. *Not run:* GitHub's behaviour for the attempts marked "not verified" (`docs.github.com` could not be fetched: the permission was not answered in the unattended run); Python 3.10 and 3.11 locally; PyPI. *Left:* local reusable workflows and composite actions (attempts 026, 027, 111) for 2d.4; unrecognised agents (`goose`, `cursor-agent`) for 2d.5; which events are an outsider's for every rule (#166); fan-in of `toJSON(needs)` over very many jobs is superlinear (about 30 s at 1,800 jobs); job ids that differ only in case; the steps fact names a command, not its target. The original specification follows. Split from 2d.2 by run `skua` (F-126). OBAAF-GHA-010 (outsider text expanded in a script), 013 (self-hosted runner for untrusted work) and 015 (release workflow started by untrusted input). *Done when* as 2d.2: each rule has a recorded attempt, every way past is a test, each fix is to the class. *Starting points (F-127):* a second reviewer attacked these three rules in run `skua` (115 attempts; 38 new ways past, 11 under the stated limits). Under `SECURITY.md` the details are published only with the fixes, so only the classes are written here, and the run that takes this slice should have them attacked again from these: OBAAF-GHA-010, outsider-written fields missing from its list (edited `changes`, a review comment's `path` and `diff_hunk`, a commit's committer on `workflow_run`, and commits' file lists, gollum page titles, a discussion's answer, `head_repository` text), a called workflow whose caller is untrusted (lower severity, and an acceptance that does not change when an untrusted caller is added), scripts other than `run` (a `docker://` step's `args`, the egress action's `run`, known script inputs), the stated limits that are cheap to follow (an `env` written as one expression, static job outputs), and every field a step expands as its facts (today the first one only); whether medium is right for commit metadata on `push`. OBAAF-GHA-013: the hosted-label pattern takes any `ubuntu-*`, `windows-*` or `macos-*` label for GitHub-hosted (use the documented list as data); a runtime selector whose candidates include `self-hosted` is informational; the selector is not in the facts; called workflows; a calling job reported as "runs on no labels"; labels with spaces. OBAAF-GHA-015: release jobs recognised by lists of actions and commands, or a production-like environment name, miss many (fail closed on capability: `id-token: write`, `packages: write`, a publishing secret, a plain-text environment); an environment named by a matrix; called workflows and composite actions; the release reason and the environment are not facts; and its severity on `pull_request` against OBAAF-GHA-001's. Also the false positive above: `pull_request_review(_comment)` counted as privileged.
- ☑ **2d.2e Release and effect jobs.** Merged as [#168](https://github.com/Obaaf/OBAAF/pull/168) by run `tattler` on 2026-11-14; split from 2d.2 (F-134); decision D-062 in `DECISIONS.md` (proposed). *What was built:* two Opus 5.5 reviewers attacked the seven rules in parallel (A: OBAAF-GHA-006, 007, 014, 123 attempts, 56 ways past, 10 false positives; B: 016, 017, 021, 022, 144 attempts, 65 ways past); every way past that is fixed is a test in `tests/test_release_effect_bypasses.py` (44 test methods), and every attempt, with what the checker reported before and after, is in [bypass-attempts.md](bypass-attempts.md#2d2e-release-and-effect-jobs). A check (`obaaf verify release`, `obaaf verify agent-run`, `obaaf proposal check`, a provenance command) counts only where its failure stops the job: `binding_commands` in `workflow.py` is an allow-list reader (F-135) that accepts the check when its line is that one command, outside compounds, in bash or sh with errexit on, and every line before it is a plain simple command or a plain `if`, `while` or `for` line, with expansions, shell options and assignments held to allow-lists; anything else is no check, and OBAAF-GHA-016 is kind `cannot-tell` when a step names a check it could not vouch for. Every upload is checked; a needed job with `continue-on-error` is no gate; `--pre-approval` is a provenance check. OBAAF-GHA-021 counts checks only before the first step that may apply, in steps that run only checks and preparing commands, and no longer takes `gh attestation verify` for a record check (F-138). OBAAF-GHA-014 and 022 count an environment only when established (`blank`, `open`, `unreadable` otherwise); 014 reports a release job holding publishing credentials (`capability`). More publishing is known; dry runs, `localhost` and `file:` targets are not uploads, and Pages or cloud deployments are not counted unless the job is a release job (F-137). OBAAF-GHA-006 knows more cache steps and follows needed jobs that restore a cache (`needed-job-restores`); 007 reads cache-mode the widest way. Steps, restoring actions, environment and write access are facts. `release-pypi.yml#publish` and `pages.yml#deploy` set `cache-mode: none`. *Run:* 1592 tests locally (1548 at the start); the new tests give 238 sub-test failures and 1 error on the old checker; CI green on the merged head `702f1f7` ([43639267074](https://github.com/Obaaf/OBAAF/actions/runs/43639267074)); on Expectrail at `3ce1c2a` (read-only copy of `.github` and `AGENTS.md`, `fresh-*` left out unread) the same 43 findings in the same states. *Review:* one Opus 5.5 sub-agent in six passes plus a last check: 10, 10, 2, 2, 1 and 0 blocking findings; a first reader that followed bash's grammar and a second that used a deny-list were each got past and replaced. *Stated, not fixed (F-136):* what earlier commands and actions do to the runner (replace `obaaf`, rewrite the policy, write `BASH_ENV` or the path file under a name the text search does not see), and a `setup-obaaf` from any repository named `obaaf` trusted by OBAAF-GHA-021 (#169). *Not run:* GitHub's behaviour where the record says "not verified" (`cache-mode` was read from GitHub's changelogs; the docs could not be fetched), PowerShell, Python 3.10 and 3.11 locally. *Left:* local reusable workflows and composite actions (2d.4); whether verified or attested files are the files uploaded; #166; a top-level `On:` key.
- ☑ **2d.2f Pins, egress and declarations.** Merged as [#178](https://github.com/Obaaf/OBAAF/pull/178) by run `shoveler` on 2026-11-15 (merge `d572930`); split from 2d.2 (F-149), issue #170 (opened by run `brambling`, whose claim lapsed unworked); decision D-065 in `DECISIONS.md` (proposed). *What was built:* two Opus 5.5 reviewers attacked the five rules in parallel (A: OBAAF-GHA-008, 009, 023, 147 attempts, 38 ways past, 3 weaker, 8 false positives, 5 tracebacks; B: OBAAF-GHA-020 and CFG-001, 160 attempts, 50 ways past, 1 false positive); every way past that is fixed is a test in `tests/test_pins_egress_declaration_bypasses.py`, and every attempt, with what the checker reported before and after, is in [bypass-attempts.md](bypass-attempts.md#2d2f-pins-egress-and-declarations). OBAAF-GHA-008: a pin is `[0-9a-f]{40}` in full, an image digest `@sha256:` and 64 hex at its end; every image is read (`image-expression`, `image-unreadable`; `${{ matrix.x }}` read value by value; an empty image is none); `$/path` is local; a `./` action is `local-from-checkout` after another repository's checkout at its path (paths resolved) and `local-after-download` after another run's artifact, a cache over it, or a clone or download into it in one command; a called workflow is never low. Every rule: a job id that is not text, two ids read as one, a merge key, or a `uses` that is not text is an error of the file; `.YML` and `.yml` files are found. OBAAF-GHA-023: values read as YAML gives them, one finding per placeholder with `where`. OBAAF-GHA-020: the firewall policy's other keys are "cannot tell" (README read 2026-11-15, F-152), the label twice is the firewall, a cleared finding names the defaults; the agent is found in each command of another action's input, a docker step, `shell`, `env` and the default shell (through wrappers and `bash -c`), not in free text; only upload-artifact itself may follow; a pattern path to the scratch directory, more detached processes, and a credential-named value outside are "cannot tell". OBAAF-CFG-001: another spelling is any name that looks like `OBAAF_LANE` or is one edit from it; the variable anywhere it is not read is `elsewhere`; Jenkins `jenkins-misnamed` and `jenkins-scripted`; two configurations or a look-alike one are a configuration error. *Run:* 1651 tests locally (1608 at the start); the first version of the new tests gave 98 sub-test failures and 4 errors on the old checker; CI green on every head of #178 (last code head `ce76e7a`, [43694125170](https://github.com/Obaaf/OBAAF/actions/runs/43694125170)); on Expectrail at `8321b17` (read-only copy of `.github` and `AGENTS.md`, `fresh-*` left out unread) the same 43 findings with the same identities. *Review:* one Opus 5.5 sub-agent in four passes: 6, 3, 2 and 0 blocking findings (quadratic patterns, false positives of the download and agent readers, unresolved paths, a document claim, empty images; `uses: ./`, URL segments, `bash -c` and wrapper options; `git clone --depth`, prose in `env`). *Not run:* GitHub's behaviour where the record says "not verified" (`.YML` files, merge keys, case-variant keys, `null:` ids, an empty container, `..` in action paths), Python 3.10 and 3.11 locally, PyPI. *Left:* #179, impostor commits, agents run by `npm run`, a called workflow or a local composite action (2d.4), post steps of earlier actions, code merged into the workspace, `git clone --ref-format`.
- ☑ **2d.2g The settings rules.** Merged as [#181](https://github.com/Obaaf/OBAAF/pull/181) by run `wryneck` on 2026-11-15 (merge `6b7d537`); split from 2d.2 (F-155), issue #180; decision D-066 in `DECISIONS.md` (proposed). Not core when claimed; the run took the core slot once the checker's `Job.triggers` needed fixing (F-157). *What was built:* two Opus 5.5 reviewers attacked the fifteen settings rules in parallel (A: OBAAF-SET-001, 002, 003, 009 to 012, 014, 015, 166 attempts, 9 ways past, 5 false positives, 2 slow; B: OBAAF-SET-004 to 008 and 013, 149 attempts, 22 ways past, 7 false positives, 2 slow); every attempt, with what the audit answered before and after, is in [bypass-attempts.md](bypass-attempts.md#2d2g-the-settings-rules), and every class fixed is a test in `tests/test_settings_bypasses.py` (62 tests). OBAAF-SET-001: an audited branch counts for its rules (no approval rule: a finding; a limit on who may push, a required deployment or a code owner's review: "cannot tell"), a backslash is a pattern, an environment named by a number is "cannot tell" (SET-002 too). SET-003: a protected secret in an environment open to every branch is high. SET-004: the GitHub Actions app as a bypass actor is high, `exempt` is wide, a classic rule that requires nothing is quiet. SET-006 to 008: Actions off, refused files and blocked events stop a job; files GitHub may refuse or not start (lockfile, `local_only`, a policy not understood, a call to such a file or a missing one), files with a checker problem (but input names), odd names, symlinks and their callers never count as reporting; names and matrix values YAML 1.2 reads otherwise are "cannot tell"; a plain skipped job reports for SET-007; a required check only `pull_request_target` reports is "cannot tell" for SET-007; a required name two jobs report is "cannot tell" for SET-008; unread Actions settings are named on the rule's line (`assumed`, F-156). SET-009/010: repository conditions other than every repository are "cannot tell". SET-012: reusable workflows' tokens. SET-015: 40-hex pins, `$/` local, lockfile "cannot tell". Checker: `Job.triggers` memoised and iterative; a cycle in `needs` narrows nothing. *Run:* 1713 tests locally (1651 at the start); CI green on the last head `85ce3ca` ([43709389428](https://github.com/Obaaf/OBAAF/actions/runs/43709389428)); `obaaf check` gives identical findings with the old and new code on this repository, `templates/`, the workflow fixtures and Expectrail at `f7bce42` (read-only copy of `.github` and `AGENTS.md`, `fresh-*` left out unread); `obaaf audit` on GitHub's recorded answers for Expectrail gives one new finding, OBAAF-SET-001 medium on `main-only` (F-159). *Review:* four passes by fresh Opus 5.5 sub-agents: 8, 4, 3 and 0 blocking findings; the last commit is wording. *Not run:* anything against GitHub itself (how a `pull_request_target` check counts, how two check runs of one name are weighed, the Actions app's actor id, `local_only`'s exact reach, policies' repository conditions are "not verified"); Python 3.11; PyPI. *Left:* #182 (`uses: ./` and `$/` calls, job ids GitHub refuses, an empty selected-actions list, a quadratic caller loop), SET-008's `if: false` false positive, `concurrency`, refusals the audit does not know, actions inside local composite actions and called workflows.
- ☑ **2d.2h The repository and instruction-file rules.** Merged as [#184](https://github.com/Obaaf/OBAAF/pull/184) by run `bittern` on 2026-11-15 (merge `05eef81`); split from 2d.2 by run `dipper` (F-160), issue #183; decision D-067 in `DECISIONS.md` (proposed). Run `dipper` did the attacks, the first fixes and two review passes; its claim lapsed when its sub-agents hit a usage limit, and `bittern` finished the draft (F-160). *What was built:* two Opus 5.5 reviewers attacked the three rules (245 attempts: 68 ways past OBAAF-REPO-001 and 002, 70 past OBAAF-INS-001 and the discovery both use, 13 false positives, 6 slow cases); every attempt, with the answer before and after, is in [bypass-attempts.md](bypass-attempts.md#2d2h-the-repository-and-instruction-file-rules), and every class fixed is a test in `tests/test_repo_instruction_bypasses.py` (83 tests). CODEOWNERS is read as GitHub documents it (line feeds, spaces and tabs, `#` after whitespace, `**` beside slashes, the owner form with older user names allowed, 3 MB, links and encodings at the three places, exact names), on a linear-time automaton with bounded comparisons. OBAAF-REPO-001 covers local actions, the lockfile, a workflow not written yet (decided from the rules) and linked workflows, and a second finding no acceptance can clear covers the gate files (CODEOWNERS and the places before it, the configuration's places, a baseline or configuration by path, the firewall policy's place); with no CODEOWNERS file nothing can be accepted. A file inside a submodule is owned by the submodule's entry. Instruction files: git's tracked files (git from an absolute `PATH` folder outside the repository, whatever the current folder; a note when a git work tree is walked), more names from the tools' own pages, any case (as `re.IGNORECASE` compares), links followed (a folder that holds the link included; a link back into a folder already entered followed again when a pattern could match beneath its name), one name kept for each file and link, limits that are errors (2,000,000 entries; 1,000,000 looked at and 100,000,000 characters of names behind links; 200,000 names; eight links in a chain). OBAAF-INS-001 by Unicode property, with Unicode 15.1's format characters listed for older Pythons, strict UTF-8, linear digests and paragraph digests for direction controls; a binary-named file is skipped only when it begins or looks binary; a file is read, scanned and reported once, with the other names it is read by. *Review:* after `dipper`'s two passes (14 and 8 blocking), four passes by fresh Opus 5.5 sub-agents in run `bittern` (9, 5, 5 and 2 blocking), then three re-checks of the last fixes by the sixth reviewer (2, 1, 0); each finding and fix is in the record. *Run:* 1797 tests locally on Python 3.13 (1713 at the start); CI green on the merged head `b40d53c` ([43762584060](https://github.com/Obaaf/OBAAF/actions/runs/43762584060)); `obaaf catalog`, `obaaf docs build --check`, `obaaf check . --fail-on medium` exit 0; Expectrail at `3c7ee5c9` (read-only copy without `fresh-*`, as a copy and as a git work tree) gives the same 44 findings, errors and notes as before the slice, byte for byte; ten cloned public repositories give the same repository and instruction-file findings as each review's code; the scan reports the same 4,271 code points on Python 3.11, 3.12 and 3.13. *Not run:* anything against GitHub (the record's "not verified" cases), Windows, Python 3.10 locally (CI runs it), PyPI. *Left:* the stated limits in the two rules' descriptions (files pulled in by reference; a link out named `docs`; only a chain's first link needs an owner; a submodule not checked out; an AGENTS.md in a folder whose name holds a newline; Python 3.10 and joiners before letters of Unicode 14); `obaaf check` runs the search twice; the Jenkinsfile discovery cases go to the Jenkins tranche.
- ☑ **2d.2i Jenkins files and credentials.** Merged as [#188](https://github.com/Obaaf/OBAAF/pull/188) by run `smew` on 2026-11-15 (merge `2174226`); split from 2d.2 (F-167), issue #187; decision D-068 in `DECISIONS.md` (proposed). *What was built:* three Opus 5.5 reviewers attacked the Jenkins adapter: A, which Jenkinsfiles are found and read (28 attempts; a safety classifier stopped its turn before OBAAF-JEN-001); B, OBAAF-JEN-013, 014 and 015 and how bindings are read (104); C, OBAAF-JEN-001, given alone after the first fixes (98). 230 attempts: 89 ways past (3 of them a lower severity), 4 false positives, 1 slow case; 86 fixed by class, 8 not fixed and stated. Every attempt, with the answer before and after, is in [bypass-attempts.md](bypass-attempts.md#2d2i-jenkins-files-and-credentials); `tests/test_jenkins_files_credentials.py` has 124 cases and 17 other tests. In a git work tree every file git lists is looked at, in any folder and at any depth, links in the repository are read under their own path and nothing outside it is read; notes say what is left out. A lone CR ends a line. Bindings are read in every form Groovy gives the same call; one the checker cannot read is a binding of unknown credentials; code reached through closures, method pointers and maps of closures runs where it is called, under the budget. OBAAF-JEN-001 knows `PR-<n>-head`, `PR-<n>-merge`, `MR-<n>` and `GIT_BRANCH`; OBAAF-JEN-013 follows a secret through copies, parameters, closures and `withEnv`, and what it cannot follow inside a binding is "unknown"; OBAAF-JEN-014's names and commands, shared with OBAAF-GHA-018, are longer and narrower where the review found noise; OBAAF-JEN-015 reads tokens built from literals and GitLab's `secretToken`. *Review:* a fourth Opus 5.5 sub-agent, six passes (8, 1, 1, 1, 1 and 0 blocking findings) and a last check of a performance-only change (0): endless expansion, a list changed after it was assigned, files read outside the repository, a slow signing scan, false positives on ordinary workflows from the shared signing lists (`ASC_KEY_ID`, `SENTRY_UPLOAD_KEY`), the wrong rule id in the docs, and four rounds on how a closure parameter holding a secret is spelled. *Run:* 1815 tests (1797 before); `obaaf catalog`, `obaaf docs build --check`, `obaaf check . --fail-on medium`; on the code before the slice 110 of the 124 cases and 15 of the 17 other tests fail; OBAAF and Expectrail at `ec4c1f55` give the same files and findings before and after; CI green on `d30fe33` ([run 43778410040](https://github.com/Obaaf/OBAAF/actions/runs/43778410040)). *Not run:* a Jenkins controller, Python 3.10 locally, PyPI; several plugin pages could not be fetched, and the cases that rest on them are "not verified". *Done when* every way past found in the attacks on these files and rules is a test and its class is fixed, or stated as a limit with the reason. It changes the Jenkins adapter, `detection.yaml`'s signing lists, their tests and their documentation.

- ☑ **2d.2k Pipeline code and pins** (core; #219). Merged as [#218](https://github.com/Obaaf/OBAAF/pull/218) by run `brant` on 2026-11-17, split from 2d.2j (F-221); decision D-079 in `DECISIONS.md` (proposed); the record is [bypass-attempts.md](bypass-attempts.md#2d2k-pipeline-code-and-pins); every change an adopter will see is in the changelog under "The Jenkins rules about pipeline code and pins attacked" and in the upgrade note. *What was tried:* an Opus 5.5 reviewer that did not write the rules wrote 85 attempts on OBAAF-JEN-004 and 008: 48 ways past, 2 weaker, 7 false positives, 2 slow. *What was built:* OBAAF-JEN-004 reads Groovy run through the script as a variable, `Eval`, Groovy shells, class loaders, script engines and JSR-223 engines (by qualified name, import alias, copy and method pointer), Job DSL in both spellings, a library step's retriever (`legacySCM(scm)`, a remote from a run-time value), a version from a workspace file, Groovy built from a parameter on any branch, and `GIT_BRANCH` compared with a pattern; literal text, `readTrusted` and `libraryResource` are not findings and a receiver that may be something else is "unknown". OBAAF-JEN-008 reads images given as values, pod specs as YAML, containerTemplates, Dockerfiles in their folders (agents, `docker.build`, a script's `docker build`; FROM, COPY --from, RUN --mount, RUN installs, ARG and ENV scope, heredocs, parser directives), container commands where a command starts, and agent CLI installs from scripts, repository scripts, requirements files and `package.json` without a lockfile, in the folder the command runs in; only an exact version or digest passes; a variable, environment value or folder is put in only when fixed to one literal, otherwise "unknown"; an image the job builds is not a registry reference; text from a file is escaped in messages; the slow patterns are linear. *Review:* nine passes by fresh Opus 5.5 sub-agents on the fixes (16, 21, 11, 8, 5, 8, 2, 2 and 1 blocking findings) and a tenth check of the last fix (none); most of the later findings were in reading a shell script's variables and folders, where each fix was followed by a case it did not foresee (F-222). 214 cases are tests in `tests/test_jenkins_code_pins.py`, 56 of the attack's 81 with another result on the code before. *Not fixed (stated in the record):* an upper-case SHA in a library version, `load` of a file just written from `readTrusted`, agents the catalogue does not name (2d.5), shell branches and `pushd -n`, a case pattern's `)`, `docker build -`, the cost of a method run under many `dir()`s. *Effect:* Expectrail and this repository byte-identical; the other Jenkins rules identical on 111 cases; the Jenkins templates' OBAAF-JEN-008 coverage is now "unknown" (F-223).
- ☐ **2d.3 Property and fuzz tests.** *Done when* the expression and workflow readers have property tests (for example: rewriting an expression in an equivalent spelling never removes a finding) that run in CI. *Positioning (1.4), decision F-19:* add a parity test to CI. A mapping file pairs zizmor audits with OBAAF rules (`template-injection` with GHA-010, `unpinned-uses` with 008, `excessive-permissions` with 009, `artipacked` with 011, `cache-poisoning` with 006). The test runs a pinned zizmor on the risky workflows, and on at least one set of workflows OBAAF's authors did not write, and fails when zizmor reports a mapped audit and OBAAF is silent, unless the mapping records why. One such case is known: on `clinejection-release` zizmor reports `artipacked` and OBAAF is silent, because GHA-011 covers agent jobs and untrusted triggers only. GHA-002, 013 and 019 have no matching zizmor audit and are compared with poutine's and CodeQL's recorded results.
- ☑ **2d.4a An agent inside a local composite action makes the job an agent job** (core; #80; split from 2d.4 by run `redpoll`, F-228). Merged as [#222](https://github.com/Obaaf/OBAAF/pull/222) by run `redpoll` on 2026-11-18; decision D-081 in `DECISIONS.md` (proposed); the guide is [checker-configuration.md](checker-configuration.md#telling-the-checker-about-your-agents); every change an adopter will see is in the changelog under "An agent inside a local composite action makes an agent job". *What changed:* a step that uses `./path` or `$/path` (and, for an agent, `.\path` or the path with space round it) puts its job in the agent lane when the action is a composite action and an agent runs in its steps; the steps are read for an agent as a job's are, with the using step's `with:` and `env`, the action's defaults, `github.action_path` and simple expressions put in, scripts found from the root and from the action's folder when the step goes there, and further local actions followed ten deep. What cannot be read through is one note; an action file the checker cannot read as YAML, and a check whose local actions cost more than its budget (30,000 units, and 100 for each using step for itself), are errors. *How it was checked:* 57 tests in `tests/test_local_composite_actions.py`; 2,078 tests locally; CI green on `3b3ad84`; this repository's report and Expectrail's at `f167f4e` byte-identical before and after. Nine review passes by one Opus 5.5 sub-agent, about 190 attempts (8, 3, 2, 3, 2, 2, 1, 1 and 1 blocking findings; the last was a figure in the documents); every class is a test (F-230). *Not run:* GitHub itself on `@`, `..` and backslashes in a local path, which metadata file wins, and whether a composite step inherits the using step's `env`; each is read the way that finds more.
- ☑ **2d.4b1 A called local reusable workflow is read with what its callers give it** (core; #80; split from 2d.4b by run `linnet`, F-233). Merged as [#224](https://github.com/Obaaf/OBAAF/pull/224) (`c40448e`); decision D-082 is proposed; the changelog's first section, the upgrading row and `docs/bypass-attempts.md` say what changed. *What changed:* `obaaf check` and `--lanes` read every workflow file before checking any; a job that calls a workflow of the repository by its path (`./`, `$/`, `.\`; with and without what follows `@`; backslashes as slashes; a candidate outside the repository dropped on its own; letter case kept; a link by its own name) gives the called workflow's jobs the events that can start it, after its `if:` and `needs`, as well as `workflow_call`. The calls are split into strongly connected components and worked out callers first; each file of a cycle gets every event that reaches any of them. A caller's event that is neither documented nor named by a rule reaches the called jobs as one stand-in. OBAAF-GHA-001, 005 and 018 on a called job carry a fact `passed`: what each caller passes for the secrets the job reads, followed to the first caller through `secrets: inherit`; each mapping indexed once; each entry bounded; past five entries or 1,000 characters, at most three entries and a digest of all. A note names each called workflow read with events its own `on` does not name. A workflow no job calls is exactly as before. *How it was checked:* 45 tests in `tests/test_local_reusable_workflows.py`; 2123 tests locally at `bcadaf5`; CI green on `5622e22` ([run 44270485616](https://github.com/Obaaf/OBAAF/actions/runs/44270485616), 11 of 11); this repository's report byte-identical; Expectrail at `2e014e11` (read-only, copy without `fresh-*`) with identical findings and lanes and one more note; every fixture and template file identical. Five review passes by Opus 5.5 sub-agents (115 attempts, then 48, then 2,000 random graphs against a brute force, a fuzz of the walk and 221 repositories old against new, then 1,000 graphs and 1,583 findings, then a check of the last code change): 4, 3, 1, 0 and 0 blocking findings. *Left, stated in D-082:* an `ignore` entry still covers any mapping; a secret picked by an expression is "any secret"; two calling jobs of one workflow share its events in the fact; an inherit chain is quadratic (about 46 s for 3,000 files); the `triggers` fact of other findings lists every documented event; GitHub's handling of `@`, backslashes, odd keys, links, its nesting limit and its events page were not read.
- ☑ **2d.4b2 A local composite action's own `uses`, uploads, caches and checkouts** (core; #225; split from 2d.4b by run `linnet`, F-233). Merged as [#226](https://github.com/Obaaf/OBAAF/pull/226) by run `stonechat` on 2026-11-18; decision D-083 is proposed; the changelog's first section, the upgrading row, the guide's section "The other rules read the action's steps too" and the section "2d.4b2" of [bypass-attempts.md](bypass-attempts.md) say what changed. *What changed:* `Job.local_steps` holds the steps of the local composite actions a job's steps use, with the using step's `with:` put into `inputs` references as expressions (a plain-text result is text, except in `if:` and `continue-on-error`; `toJSON(inputs)` and the like, and a `with:` written as one value, are every input, in the note), `github.action_path`, and the using step's `env`, `if:` and `continue-on-error`; nested local actions are followed. `WorkflowRules.through_local_actions` reruns every rule on a copy in which each using step is followed by those steps and the job is in the lanes they put it in (F-236); findings on an action's step are added, one per step; a first-pass finding of the same case on a job's step or job is replaced by the second pass's, never less severe or nearer to cleared (F-237); another case is added where the first pass has none of the rule there as severe, or the copy's job is in a lane the job is not; OBAAF-GHA-023 reads each use of an action's steps as written. Bounds per job (1,000 steps, 2,000,000 characters) and per check (10,000, 10,000,000), and one input-made text over 200,000 characters, are errors (F-238). `_bash_env` and `_verify_kind` are worked out once per job and step (990 steps: 98 s before, under a second after). This repository's `release-pypi.yml#publish` gets OBAAF-GHA-016 "cannot tell", accepted in `.github/obaaf.yml` for Sean to confirm (F-239, item 64). *How it was checked:* 41 tests in `tests/test_local_action_steps.py`, among them a property over 14 cases against the same steps written in the job, which 13 of them failed before; 2,164 tests locally; CI green on `0d407e6` (run 44289720847) and on the merged head; Expectrail at `cd57180` (read-only, a copy without `fresh-*`) byte-identical in JSON, text and `--lanes`, with 730 steps of local actions read; every fixture, template and demo file identical. Four review passes by Opus 5.5 sub-agents (115 attempts, 66 and a fuzz of 1,458 malformed actions, 37, and a re-check): 6, 6, 3 and 0 blocking findings, every class a test. *Left, stated in D-083:* an action's `outputs` read in the job; the secrets rules, which read the job's own text; OBAAF-GHA-015 `capability` beside the release case; OBAAF-GHA-014 and 016 twice for a job that publishes itself after a publishing action; placeholders in input defaults; a "cannot tell" finding's digest changes after any local action before it; nesting past ten is a note; GitHub's pages on a composite step's `env`, `if:` and `secrets` were read only through a summary.
- ☑ **2d.4b3a What a called local workflow is given** (core; #228; split from 2d.4b3 by run `wryneck`, F-240). Merged as [#229](https://github.com/Obaaf/OBAAF/pull/229) by run `wryneck` on 2026-11-18; decision D-084 is proposed; the changelog's first section, two upgrading rows and the section "2d.4b3a" of [bypass-attempts.md](bypass-attempts.md) say what changed. *What changed:* in `_give_callers`, before a group's calls are followed, `_put_in_caller_inputs` replaces each `inputs` reference outside `on` of a called workflow that names one (not a job's or step's `name`, nor a job's `runs-on`, F-243) with `format()` of the reference as written and each value a calling job hands (`with:`, else the default; F-241), worked out where the caller runs: the caller's own contexts as `caller.<context>`, and its matrix's values and needed jobs' outputs followed to three levels (`_CallerValues`); callers first, so a chain carries the first caller's value; in place, all at once. `Job.granted_by_callers` gives OBAAF-GHA-003's and 004's `inherited` findings a bounded fact `granted` (`_callers_fact`, shared with `passed`). Bounds (F-242): 1,000,000 characters in one text, 5,000,000 for the check, 500,000 of values for one input, 1,000,000 places in one workflow; `env` and `defaults` counted per step, local actions' steps included once known (`_check_caller_input_reads`, which puts the values back past the bound); each an error. *Before* (reproduced on `fdb1754`): a `pull_request_target` caller handing the head to `inputs.ref` was OBAAF-GHA-002 "cannot tell" at medium, an outsider's text handed to an input and expanded in a script was nothing, and an accepted `inherited` covered a caller that grants `contents: write`. *Now:* OBAAF-GHA-002 high, OBAAF-GHA-010 high, and the acceptance stops matching. *Review:* seven passes by two Opus 5.5 sub-agents that wrote none of it (one for passes 1 and 2, a fresh one for 3 to 7): 2, 2, 6, 2, 2, 1 and 0 blocking findings, about 120, 120, 352 and 406 case repositories compared with the code before; no finding of the code before was lost or lowered. Most were costs (128 s, 1.9 GB, a `MemoryError` traceback, 147 s, 57 s, 120 s), and the rest false findings or errors on ordinary shapes (`needs.*.result`, the fork-safe runner selector, `${PREFIX}` in a value ending an `if:`'s narrowing, a step name changing identities, a "run this script" workflow failing a bound) and ways past (the caller's matrix and outputs, `include` as one expression). Every class is a test: 60 in `tests/test_called_workflow_inputs.py`. *Run:* 2,224 tests locally at `1a2ba72`; CI on #229 green on 9 of 11 checks at `1a2ba72` ([run 44327799496](https://github.com/Obaaf/OBAAF/actions/runs/44327799496)), the two macOS jobs queued without a runner (F-244); `obaaf catalog`, `obaaf docs build --check` and `obaaf check . --fail-on medium` exit 0; this repository's report and Expectrail's at `4fd1a758` (read-only, without `fresh-*`, its CI command, text, JSON and `--lanes`) byte-identical before and after. *Not run:* GitHub's pages on reusable workflows (the readings are the wider ones); PyPI; Windows and macOS locally. *Left, in D-084:* `runs-on` and names not put in; other rules read a called job's undeclared permissions as unknown; a value followed past three levels, and a step's output inside a needed job's output; an agent CLI named only through an input; after a put-back, lanes from the values put in stay; one 10 KB repository built to pass the bound fails with an error; 2d.4b2's facts that quote a local action's inputs are written differently.
- ☑ **2d.4b3b What a called local workflow does** (core; #228; the rest of 2d.4b3, F-240). Merged as [#230](https://github.com/Obaaf/OBAAF/pull/230) by run `siskin` on 2026-11-18; decision D-085 is proposed; the changelog's first section, five upgrading rows and the section "2d.4b3b" of [bypass-attempts.md](bypass-attempts.md) say what changed. *What changed:* `check` and `--lanes` classify every workflow, with its scripts and local actions, before any rule runs (`_prepare_workflows`; what classifying a file reports is reported with it, in the same order), and `rules.give_call_effects` then works out, callees first over `_give_callers`' groups (a cycle as one, the wider way), what each calling job's called jobs do (`CallEffects`, bounded `CallSummary`s of distinct entries with a digest). A calling job is put in the release lane when a called job is in it ("calls X, whose job 'j' is a release job (...)"); such a job gets no OBAAF-GHA-006, 014, 015 or 016 of its own (the called jobs carry them with its events), its OBAAF-GHA-015 `capability` is kind `capability-call`, and it gets `needed-job-restores` when it needs a job that restores and a called release job downloads. A declared release calling job gets `called-restores` (high) for restoring steps of called jobs that are not release jobs, through jobs between that release only through their call, unless a cache-mode on the way forbids it (F-245). A needed job that calls restores what any job it calls restores. A called job whose every caller in the repository forbids restoring, in a workflow only `workflow_call` starts and that no job calls by name, has a "cannot tell" control on OBAAF-GHA-006. Decided: a call by `owner/repo/<path>@ref` is not matched, with a note where the path exists here; a called job's `if: github.event_name == '<event>'` adds the event for a caller in another repository, not narrowed away by a needed job that such a caller may run (memoised per job and event). A lane a call gives does not make a stray declaration redundant (OBAAF-CFG-001 stays high). `Job.cache_mode` and OBAAF-GHA-007 read huge numbers with `as_text` (a traceback the code before had for release and `pull_request_target` jobs). *Before* (on `db7fd5a`): attempt 026's and 103's calling jobs were "trusted (default)"; 034's declared release job had only the low `no-cache-mode`; 035's called release job was "new" though its only caller sets `cache-mode: none`. *Now:* a release job; `called-restores` high; "cannot tell". *Review:* four passes by two Opus 5.5 sub-agents that wrote none of it: one for passes 1 and 2 (about 90 and 100 attempts; 3 and 2 blocking findings), a fresh one for 3 and 4 (about 80 comparisons, 30 sweeps and 13 cost runs, then about 60 attempts; 3 and 0 blocking). Blocking: a high `called-restores` from called jobs that do not release (the same jobs in one workflow give nothing); OBAAF-CFG-001 lowered; a traceback; a high `needed-job-restores` from a called job that does not release; a workflow between hiding restores; a cost (a 79 KB file took 399 s, now 2.6 s against 0.75 s before the slice); a needed calling job whose called release job restores; the order of jobs changing `called-restores`. Every class is a test: 54 in `tests/test_called_workflow_effects.py`. *Run:* 2,278 tests locally at `1d4928b` (the last commit, `5fda16d`, changes wording only); CI on #230 green at `699c480` ([run 44345560867](https://github.com/Obaaf/OBAAF/actions/runs/44345560867)) and at `5fda16d`, all 11 checks ([run 44354634789](https://github.com/Obaaf/OBAAF/actions/runs/44354634789)); `obaaf catalog`, `obaaf docs build --check` and `obaaf check . --fail-on medium` exit 0; this repository's report and Expectrail's at `5aa941f` (read-only, without `fresh-*`, its CI command; text, JSON and `--lanes`) byte-identical before and after, and 29 template, demo and example workflow files identical; SARIF's rule help for OBAAF-GHA-006 changes with its description. *Not run:* GitHub's pages on reusable workflows and `cache-mode` (the changelog of 2026-09-10, as in 2d.2e); PyPI; Windows and macOS locally. *Left, in D-085 and #231:* the other rules that read a called job's undeclared permissions (OBAAF-GHA-009, 011, 015's capability, the egress controls) could read the callers' grant; whether a private repository lets others call its workflows (`obaaf audit` could read it); a call's outputs; the jobs that need a job whose `if:` names an event for an outside caller are not given it; `a/b//...@r` is not a call by name; past 2,000 entries a fact can depend on the order of parts; adversarial files of many jobs, needs and events cost about three times what they did, linearly.
- ☑ **2d.4 Local reusable workflows and composite actions** (split into 2d.4a, done, and 2d.4b, on 2026-11-18; 2d.4b split into 2d.4b1 and 2d.4b2, done, and 2d.4b3, the same day; 2d.4b3 split into 2d.4b3a, done, and 2d.4b3b, by run `wryneck`, F-240; 2d.4b3b done by run `siskin`, which left the triage below as row 2d.4c, F-246; 2d.4c done by run `whinchat` as [#233](https://github.com/Obaaf/OBAAF/pull/233), which completes 2d.4, F-249; #80 closed). *Done when* a job that reaches an agent or a secret through `uses: ./...` is read through, or reported as not read, and the repository's own action files (`action.yml`) are read. Start by triaging what zizmor and Octoscan reported on OBAAF's own repository in slice 1.1 (seven and four findings; listed in [landscape.md](landscape.md), not yet judged). *From 2d.2b (F-124):* two ways past OBAAF-GHA-005 and 003 belong here. The caller of a local reusable agent workflow can map any secret onto the parameter the agent reads (`secrets: {MODEL_KEY: ${{ secrets.PROD_DEPLOY_KEY }}}`), and the finding on the called job names only the parameter, so changing the mapping raises nothing; and an agent inside a local composite action (`uses: ./.github/actions/x`) does not make the job an agent job, so a job with `contents: write` and `id-token: write` gets neither OBAAF-GHA-003 nor 004. Both are described in [bypass-attempts.md](bypass-attempts.md). *From 2d.2c (F-126):* a `pull_request_target` workflow that calls a local reusable workflow which checks out `github.event.pull_request.head.sha` or `inputs.ref` (passed the head by the caller) gets no OBAAF-GHA-002 and no OBAAF-GHA-011, because the called file is read with its own trigger, `workflow_call`; and a local composite action given the head SHA as an input checks it out unread. Reading a called workflow with its callers' triggers, and a composite action with its inputs substituted, would fix both, and the same for OBAAF-GHA-010, 013 and 015 (2d.2d).
- ☑ **2d.4c Triage what zizmor and Octoscan reported on OBAAF's own repository** (not core; #80). Merged as [#233](https://github.com/Obaaf/OBAAF/pull/233) by run `whinchat` on 2026-11-18 (decisions F-247 to F-249; no decision for `DECISIONS.md`: nothing the tools do changes). *What was built:* a section "Triage, 2026-11-18" in [landscape.md](landscape.md#triage-2026-11-18) judges the eleven findings of slice 1.1's run on `461090e`, against the same places on `flagship` at `3ecb7c1` (unchanged but for line numbers). Two were right and are fixed: zizmor's `dependabot-cooldown`, twice; `.github/dependabot.yml` now sets `cooldown: default-days: 7` on both updaters (GitHub's default is three days), held by `tests/test_dependabot_cooldown.py`; it takes effect when it reaches `main` (F-247). Nine are kept with their reasons: `github-env` on `actions/setup-obaaf/action.yml` (a `GITHUB_PATH` line no outsider can steer; its effect on the release job is the accepted OBAAF-GHA-016 "cannot tell" of item 64); `dangerous-triggers` on `pages.yml` (its `build` job runs only after a successful CI push to `main` or by hand on `main`, and checks out tested `main`); `self-repository` and Octoscan's `local-action`, three times each, on `uses: ./actions/setup-obaaf` (in `release-pypi.yml` `./` must stay, so that the verifier comes from the default branch and not the tag; `local-action` is Octoscan saying it did not read the action); Octoscan's `dangerous-checkout` on `release-pypi.yml` (the ref is the default branch's name). The same patterns in files added since were read, not scanned. Found: an outsider's text written to `GITHUB_ENV` or `GITHUB_PATH` through an environment variable gets no finding and is not in OBAAF-GHA-010's limits (issue [#232](https://github.com/Obaaf/OBAAF/issues/232), reproduced with the body, the title and `GITHUB_PATH`); a parity mapping for 2d.3 is written. Three older sentences of the landscape that said OBAAF does not read local actions have dated notes. *Run:* 2,279 tests locally on `ebef113` (the last commit is wording); CI 11 of 11 green on the merged head `1e30589` ([run 44374689793](https://github.com/Obaaf/OBAAF/actions/runs/44374689793)); `obaaf catalog`, `obaaf docs build --check`, `obaaf check . --fail-on medium` and `site/build.py` exit 0; OBAAF's report on this repository unchanged. *Review:* three passes by one Opus 5.5 sub-agent that wrote none of it (3, 1 and 1 blocking findings: the changelog's count, FR11 cited for the wrong requirement, Octoscan's rules described beyond the evidence; then the pull request's description, twice); every one fixed. *Not run:* zizmor and Octoscan (F-248); GitHub's Dependabot reference beyond a summary of its `cooldown` section; whether Dependabot accepts the file (it is validated on `main`). *Left:* #232 (core); removing setup-obaaf's `GITHUB_PATH` line (item 64); the step name "Set up OBAAF from the default branch (FR11)" in `release-pypi.yml`, which names the wrong requirement (item 67); Scorecard's local scores, not among the eleven.
- ◐ **2d.5 Agent tools: named, pinned, and not set to unsafe.** *Split on 2026-11-18 by run `whinchat` (F-250): part 3 is 2d.5a, done (entry below); part 1 is 2d.5b and part 2 is 2d.5c, rows in the table; this entry stays their specification.* Found in slice 1.1: an agent's command-line tool installed by piping a download into a shell, or by a package manager without a fixed version, inside a `run` step. Agent control R6 asks for agent tools to be pinned; GHA-008 reads only actions and images. *Done when* an agent job that installs its tool this way is reported, with a test built from the pattern poutine found, and the decision whether to detect it in OBAAF or to take it from poutine's output is recorded against the positioning in 1.4. *Positioning (1.4), decisions F-22 and F-24:* this slice now has three parts, and all three are needed for it to be done. (1) Agent tools become data in the style of signature files (one entry per tool: action names, program and package, the inputs that carry a prompt, and the settings to report), covering at least the tools ci-agent-audit recognised at its commit `4a88ee6`. (2) New rules, with new ids, report settings that the tool's own documentation warns about: for example `safety-strategy: unsafe` and `allow-users: "*"` on `openai/codex-action`, `allowed_non_write_users: "*"` and `allowed_bots: "*"` on `anthropics/claude-code-action`, and flags such as `--dangerously-skip-permissions`. Each rule cites the sentence of documentation it rests on; a setting that only another scanner's data calls dangerous is not reported. Fixtures are written from that documentation, not copied from another project. (3) An agent job that installs its tool by piping a download into a shell, or without a fixed version, is reported by OBAAF itself, for agent jobs only (control R6); the same pattern in any other job stays poutine's to report. The pull request records how OBAAF compares with ci-agent-audit on the same fixtures if a session can run it, or says it could not. Not covered by this slice and not matched by OBAAF: ci-agent-audit's checks for outsider text in the prompt, unpinned MCP servers, and an agent told to fetch issue text; until OBAAF has them the adoption guide says so (slice 3.4).
- ☑ **2d.5a Agent tools pinned: OBAAF-GHA-025** (core; #81, open for 2d.5b and 2d.5c). Merged as [#234](https://github.com/Obaaf/OBAAF/pull/234) by run `whinchat` on 2026-11-18 (decisions F-250 and F-251; D-086 in `DECISIONS.md`, proposed). *What was built:* a new rule, OBAAF-GHA-025, high, for agent jobs only (R6, G2.1). Kind `piped-download`: a download (`curl`, `wget`, `irm`, `iwr`, `Invoke-WebRequest`, `Invoke-RestMethod`, `aria2c`) piped to a program that runs its input (a shell, PowerShell, `iex`, Python, Node and others, after `sudo -u`, `env`, `exec`, `timeout 60`, `busybox`, or a program named by a variable; `tee` passes it on; a program given a script file reads it as data), or inside `$(...)`, backticks, `<(...)`, `(...)` or after `<<<` where what precedes it evaluates or interprets, whatever tool it installs; read by a new reader, `_downloads_run`, on the whole line (quotes not read). Kind `agent-cli`: an agent CLI the catalogue names installed or run by a package manager with no exact version, at a range, a tag or a version set at run time, updated (in that step or a later one), or by its own install script (named by its URL), read by OBAAF-JEN-008's reader with substitutions and quoted separators handled. Kind `too-long`, medium, over 400,000 characters. Read in every script a step runs, local composite actions' steps and a `docker://` step's entrypoint and args included; expressions put in as the runner does; literal env values put in unless a nearer `env` is an expression or an earlier step writes `GITHUB_ENV`. OBAAF-JEN-008's reader is linear on many pipes and many installers (97 s and 35 s before). A package named at run time is not reported (F-251). *Run:* 2,312 tests locally on `c217588`; CI 11 of 11 green there ([run 44393761593](https://github.com/Obaaf/OBAAF/actions/runs/44393761593)); `obaaf catalog`, `obaaf docs build --check`, `site/build.py` exit 0; this repository's JSON report byte-identical, templates and demo unchanged; Expectrail at `8868a131` (read-only, without `fresh-*`): exactly 12 new high findings (the 10 `curl | bash` installs poutine and Semgrep reported, and 2 `npm install -g @anthropic-ai/claude-code` with no version), every other finding and `--lanes` identical; release 0.3.0 reports neither case. *Review:* three passes by one Opus 5.5 sub-agent that wrote none of it (11, 1 and 0 blocking findings): ways past through other pipe and substitution forms, quoted separators, variables, installs inside substitutions, `docker://` joining, a later update, a repeated command, `GITHUB_ENV` and `env` expressions; false positives from `named-at-run-time` and a pinned `${{ env.X }}`; two quadratic costs; then the package case on text, now stated and tested. *Not run:* ci-agent-audit and poutine (PyPI and github.com downloads unreachable from the session); Windows and macOS locally. *Left:* 2d.5b and 2d.5c; a `GITHUB_ENV` write anywhere makes later env-pinned versions unknown (wider); npm scopes in another case; `too-long` findings in one job differ only by line; `fetch`, `http`, `gh api` and `python -c` downloads; a line of tens of thousands of downloads is as slow as before the slice (#179).
- ☑ **2d.5b Agent tools as data, and the agent rules name the agent** (core; #81, open for 2d.5c). Merged as [#235](https://github.com/Obaaf/OBAAF/pull/235) by run `bunting` on 2026-11-18 (merge `05c512e`; decisions F-256 to F-260; D-087 in `DECISIONS.md`, proposed). *What was built:* `agent_tools` in `detection.yaml`, 23 entries (`id`, name, `read`, `sources`, `actions` with their `prompt_inputs`, `commands`, `programs`, `prompt_flags`, `packages` by npm and PyPI, `installs`, `install_urls`, `settings` empty for 2d.5c, and `carried`); `src/obaaf/agent_tools.py` builds `agent_actions`, `agent_commands`, `agent_packages`, `agent_installs` and `agent_install_urls` from them in `catalog.load()`, and `Catalog.problems()` refuses a bad entry or a list written by hand. Every tool of ci-agent-audit's 20 signature files at `4a88ee6` has an entry; each fact the slice added was read from the tool's own action file or README (shallow clones at named commits) or documentation page, and facts kept from the old lists that no source read shows are under `carried` (F-256). Added: 18 action prefixes, 16 command patterns, 8 packages, 8 install-script patterns (one widens OpenCode's); five npm packages join 2c.8's install-only list (F-258). New fact `agents` on OBAAF-GHA-003, 004, 005, 012, 020 and 025 (`agent_tools.named_agents`: entries found in the job's steps, its local actions' steps, a called workflow by an agent action's prefix and its default shell; `agents.actions: ...`/`agents.commands: ...` for configured ones), left out where none is named (F-257). A job's own `defaults.run.shell` is the only default shell read (F-260). `matches_any` asks whether any agent pattern matches through each pattern's needle (F-259). The guide's section "The agent tools the checker knows" lists every entry and what is not covered. *Run:* 2,346 tests locally on `cad309a`; CI 11 of 11 green there ([run 44417236160](https://github.com/Obaaf/OBAAF/actions/runs/44417236160)); `obaaf catalog`, `obaaf docs build --check`, `site/build.py` exit 0; this repository's text and JSON reports byte-identical; Expectrail at `6794fd2c` (read-only copy without `bugs/fresh`): text and `--lanes` byte-identical, six JSON findings gain `agents: [claude-code]`. *Review:* six passes by one Opus 5.5 sub-agent that wrote none of it (2, 1, 1, 0, 0, 0 blocking): Copilot's install-then-confined job turned `not met`; facts claimed read that were carried; three, then one, sentences that did not match the code; then the cost changes, checked against trying each pattern 211,800 times with no difference. *Not run:* ci-agent-audit; CodeRabbit's repositories (a sign-in was asked for); Python 3.10 locally (CI ran it). *Left:* 2d.5c; docker images that run an agent; command forms the tools' documentation did not give where read; text in an `echo` or comment is read as run (as before, now with more names); #236 (`agent-approval-check` makes an agent job); #237 (Jenkins' agent words).
- ☑ **2d.5c1 Agent actions given a setting their own documentation warns about: OBAAF-GHA-026** (core; #81, open for 2d.5c2). Merged as [#244](https://github.com/Obaaf/OBAAF/pull/244) by run `twite` on 2026-11-19 (merge `b694cf6`; decisions F-264 and F-267; D-088 in `DECISIONS.md`, proposed; #245 opened). *What was built:* `settings` in each agent tool's entry in `detection.yaml` (id, action, input, `equals` or `set: true`, severity, date read, the documentation's words quoted exactly, and the file at a commit with its lines), checked by `agent_tools.problems`; filled on 2026-11-19 from `openai/codex-action` at `4aac6da` (`safety-strategy: unsafe` high, `read-only` medium, `allow-users: "*"` high) and `anthropics/claude-code-action` at `5a85689` and `anthropics/claude-code-base-action` at `78702a3` (`allowed_non_write_users: "*"` high, any other value medium, `allowed_bots: "*"` medium, `show_full_output: true` medium), each quote checked word for word against a clone at that commit. New rule OBAAF-GHA-026: one finding per step and input, the first setting that matches; facts action, input, value and `setting`; the words and source shown, not facts. A value is trimmed of what Python or JavaScript trims (U+FEFF included) and compared in any case; "cannot tell" (case `computed`, at most medium) for an expression, a list or mapping, a control, format, separator, private-use or unassigned character inside, and a `with:` that is neither a mapping nor empty; the fact of such a finding is the text (its digest beyond 200 characters) or the kind of a list or mapping. Local composite actions are read through the existing second pass. Agent controls R2 and R7. New risky fixture, guide table, upgrade row, changelog, site paragraph. *Review:* five passes by one Opus 5.5 sub-agent that wrote none of it (5, 1, 1, 1 and 0 blocking): `with:` as one expression gave nothing; U+FEFF around `*` and a NUL hid the value; a "cannot tell" fact cut to 60 characters let an acceptance of `drop-sudo` cover `unsafe`; the JSON fact raised on mixed keys; building it expanded every YAML alias (702 bytes took 54 s and 5 GB); a `uses:` with an empty segment or a backslash is not matched by the shared step reader (#245, stated as not read). *Run:* 2,377 tests on `7b405bc`; `obaaf catalog`, `obaaf docs build --check`, `site/build.py` exit 0; this repository's text and JSON reports byte-identical; Expectrail at `a248982` (read-only copy without `fresh-*`) text and JSON byte-identical (it uses neither action); CI 11 of 11 green on `7b405bc` ([run 44437864493](https://github.com/Obaaf/OBAAF/actions/runs/44437864493)) and on the merge of `flagship` into it. *Not run:* ci-agent-audit; GitHub itself on the bypass forms (what the actions read was taken from their source). *Left:* 2d.5c2; `display_report`, `ACTIONS_STEP_DEBUG`, keys in clear and these actions inside another repository's composite action or reusable workflow (stated in the rule); #245; on files of hundreds of steps aliasing a 1 MB string the rule adds 30 to 45% to an 80 s check (linear; a cache by `id(raw)` would remove it).
- ☑ **2d.5c2 The rest of 2d.5c** (core; #81, closed). *Split on 2026-11-19 by run `redstart` (F-272): 2d.5c2a, 2d.5c2b and 2d.5c2c are done (entries below); this entry stays their specification.* *Done when* the settings that agent tools' own documentation warns about are reported when given as command-line options, in an action's argument input (`claude_args`, `codex-args`) or in a `run` script that runs the tool (`--dangerously-skip-permissions`, Codex's `--dangerously-bypass-approvals-and-sandbox` and `--sandbox danger-full-access` if its own documentation warns, Gemini's `--yolo` if its documentation warns), and the other 21 entries' actions have their documented settings filled the same way, each citing its sentence from the tool's own files at a commit (or saying the tool documents none); a value the checker cannot read is "cannot tell"; a sub-agent that did not write it has tried to get past it; and the PR says how OBAAF compares with ci-agent-audit on the same fixtures if a session can run it, or that it could not.
- ☑ **2d.5c2a Agent CLIs run with an option their own documentation warns about** (core; #81, open for 2d.5c2b and 2d.5c2c). Merged as [#255](https://github.com/Obaaf/OBAAF/pull/255) by run `redstart` on 2026-11-19 (merge `4cd886e`; decisions F-272 to F-274; D-090 in `DECISIONS.md`, proposed; #254 opened). *What was built:* `flags` in an agent tool's entry in `detection.yaml` (id, `options`, `equals`, severity, date, the words quoted exactly, the file at a commit with its lines), `args_inputs` on an action, both checked by `agent_tools.problems`; a new module `src/obaaf/checker/agent_flags.py` (a bash lexer, the "cannot tell" rules and the widest reading); OBAAF-GHA-026 gains three kinds: `option` (in `claude_args` of claude-code-action and its base action, and `codex-args` of codex-action, each read as its action reads it), `command-option` (wherever a step runs the program or package: `run`, `shell`, github-script, the egress action's command, another input, `env` value or `docker://` command line) and `command-computed` ("cannot tell", at most medium). Read on 2026-11-19: Claude Code's `--dangerously-skip-permissions`, `--allow-dangerously-skip-permissions`, `--permission-mode bypassPermissions` (high, from claude-code-action at `5a85689`), Codex's `--dangerously-bypass-approvals-and-sandbox`/`--yolo` (high) and `--dangerously-bypass-hook-trust` (medium, from the CLI's help at `333ea31`), Gemini CLI's `--yolo`, `-y`, `--y`, `--approval-mode yolo`, `--approvalMode yolo` (medium, from `3aa930d`). A bash or sh script is read as bash splits it (quotes, `$'...'`, heredocs, `case`, known variables, nested scripts, git settings, aliases, `trap`, interpreters, pipes into a shell, quoted command lines given to other commands; the program wherever a command starts and in the arguments of any command not on a short data list) and the widest way; in a step that names the tool, what it cannot read in the program's command, or a command it cannot name, is "cannot tell". *Review:* two Opus 5.5 sub-agents that wrote none of it: A, adversarial with bash and stub CLIs, four passes (about 95, 75, 4 and 3 ways past); B, code, claims, cost and mutations, six passes (4, 9, 5, 2, 1, 1 blocking, then none at `aad6df6`). Every class is a test (58 in `tests/test_agent_tool_flags.py`) or a stated limit. *Run:* 2,435 tests at `aad6df6`, OK; `obaaf catalog`, `obaaf docs build --check`, `site/build.py` exit 0; this repository's text and JSON reports byte-identical to `df5c77b`; Expectrail at `90ace87` (read-only copy without `fresh-*`, its CI command) byte-identical (two intermediate heads were not: a `[[` test and a Python heredoc printing "Claude Code" each made a false "cannot tell"; both fixed and tested); `test_local_composite_actions` 17.2 s on base and head; CI 11 of 11 green on `aad6df6` ([run 44466745438](https://github.com/Obaaf/OBAAF/actions/runs/44466745438)).
